attn_head_mask=attn_head_mask,\\n            phase_token_indices=phase_token_indices,\\n        )\\n        ppl_kv = self.forward_with_sparse_config(\\n            input_ids, kv_mask=kv_mask,\\n            phase_token_indices=phase_token_indices,\\n        )\\n        ppl_both = self.forward_with_sparse_config(\\n            input_ids, attn_head_mask=attn_head_mask, kv_mask=kv_mask,\\n            phase_token_indices=phase_token_indices,\\n        )\\n\\n        r_attn = ppl_attn / ppl_dense if ppl_dense > 0 else float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n        r_kv = ppl_kv / ppl_dense if ppl_dense > 0 else float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n        r_both = ppl_both / ppl_dense if ppl_dense > 0 else float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n        ii = r_both - r_attn - r_kv + 1\\n\\n        num_tokens = len(phase_token_indices) if phase_token_indices else seq_len\\n\\n        return InteractionResult(\\n            ii_value=ii,\\n            ppl_dense=ppl_dense,\\n            ppl_attn_sparse=ppl_attn,\\n            ppl_kv_sparse=ppl_kv,\\n            ppl_both_sparse=ppl_both,\\n            r_attn=r_attn,\\n            r_kv=r_kv,\\n            r_both=r_both,\\n            is_super_additive=ii > 0,\\n            sparsity_level=sparsity_level,\\n            num_tokens=num_tokens,\\n        )\\n\\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\\n    # Per-phase Interaction Index\\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n    def compute_per_phase_ii(\\n        self,\\n        input_ids: torch.Tensor,\\n        phases: List,\\n        sparsity_level: float = 0.3,\\n    ) -> Dict[str, InteractionResult]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute II separately for each phase (reasoning / execution / transition).\\n\\n        Args:\\n            phases: List of Phase objects with .name and .token_indices attributes.\\n            sparsity_level: Fraction of heads to mask / KV positions to evict.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Dict mapping phase name to its InteractionResult.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        results: Dict[str, InteractionResult] = {}\\n\\n        for phase in phases:\\n            result = self.compute_interaction_index(\\n                input_ids,\\n                sparsity_level=sparsity_level,\\n                phase_token_indices=phase.token_indices,\\n            )\\n            result.phase_name = phase.name\\n            results[phase.name] = result\\n\\n        return results\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Bootstrap CI for Interaction Index\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef bootstrap_interaction_index(\\n    ii_samples: List[float],\\n    n_resamples: int = 1000,\\n    ci_level: float = 0.95,\\n    seed: int = 42,\\n) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Bootstrap confidence interval for the mean II.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        ii_samples: Per-problem II values for a given (phase, level).\\n        n_resamples: Number of bootstrap resamples.\\n        ci_level: Confidence level (default 0.95 → 95% CI).\\n        seed: Random seed for reproducibility.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        (mean, ci_low, ci_high) using percentile method.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    samples = np.array(ii_samples)\\n    n = len(samples)\\n    boot_means = np.empty(n_resamples)\\n    for i in range(n_resamples):\\n        idx = rng.randint(0, n, size=n)\\n        boot_means[i] = samples[idx].mean()\\n    alpha = 1.0 - ci_level\\n    ci_low = float(np.percentile(boot_means, 100 * alpha / 2))\\n    ci_high = float(np.percentile(boot_means, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2)))\\n    return float(samples.mean()), ci_low, ci_high\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.51}}"

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# PACS Profiling — full MVP pipeline (v4: importance-based masking).\\n#\\n# End-to-end:\\n#   1. Load causal LM + MATH-500 (stratified 50 problems)\\n#   2. Generate reasoning traces, segment into phases (quantile-based)\\n#   3. Per problem: dense forward with output_attentions to get CE losses\\n#      + head importance (Frobenius norm) + KV importance (cumulative attention)\\n#   4. For each sparsity level (10%, 20%):\\n#      - Build importance-based masks (prune least important heads/KV positions)\\n#      - Compute per-phase perplexity under 4 conditions (dense/attn/kv/both)\\n#      - Compute per-phase Interaction Index\\n#   5. Bootstrap 95% CI per (phase, level)\\n#   6. Pass/fail judgment (CI overlap + sign reversal, OR logic)\\n#   7. Output structured JSON\\n#\\n# Usage:\\n#   python -m pacs.profiling.run_profiling \\\\\\n#     --model_path &lt;HF model ID or path> \\\\\\n#     --data_path &lt;MATH-500 JSON> \\\\\\n#     --output_dir ./profiling_output\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\nfrom itertools import combinations\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy import stats as scipy_stats\\nimport torch\\n\\nfrom ..utils.data_utils import stratified_sample_math500\\nfrom ..utils.metrics import compute_perplexity, ppl_from_cached_losses\\nfrom .interaction_index import InteractionIndexCalculator, _HeadMaskHook, bootstrap_interaction_index\\nfrom .phase_segmentation import PhaseSegmenter\\nfrom .alt_segmentation import segment_by_quantile, to_phase_annotation\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef load_model_and_tokenizer(model_path: str, device: str = \\\"cuda\\\"):\\n    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading tokenizer from %s\\\", model_path)\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading model from %s\\\", model_path)\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        model_path,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.float16,\\n        device_map=device,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\",\\n    )\\n    model.eval()\\n    return model, tokenizer\\n\\n\\ndef load_data(data_path: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loading data from %s\\\", data_path)\\n    with open(data_path) as f:\\n        data = ppl_dense = ppl_from_cached_losses(dense_ce_losses, phase_token_indices, seq_len)\\n\\n    ppl_attn = ii_calc.forward_with_sparse_config(\\n        input_ids, attn_head_mask=attn_head_mask,\\n        phase_token_indices=phase_token_indices,\\n    )\\n    ppl_kv = ii_calc.forward_with_sparse_config(\\n        input_ids, kv_mask=kv_mask,\\n        phase_token_indices=phase_token_indices,\\n    )\\n Accumulators: {phase_name: {level_str: {condition: [ppl_values]}}}\\n    ppl_per_phase_level: Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[str, List[float]]]] = defaultdict(\\n        lambda: defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))\\n    )\\n    # Phase token counts\\n    phase_token_counts: Dict[str, List[int]] = defaultdict(list)\\n    phase_segment_counts: Dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)\\n\\n    # P1 diagnostics accumulators\\n    all_problem_diagnostics: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\\n    all_sparsity_values: List[float] = []\\n\\n    # Phase segmentation on the generation trace (quantile-based)\\n        gen_sparsity = np.array(step_sparsities)\\n        quant_seg = segment_by_quantile(gen_sparsity, n_groups=3)\\n        annotation = to_phase_annotation(\\n            quant_seg.assignments,\\n            quant_seg.group_names,\\n            gen_sparsity,\\n            offset=0,\\n            min_phase_length=args.min_phase_length,\\n        )\\n\\n        #       model_path,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.float16,\\n        device_map=device,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        attn_implementation=\\\"eager\\\",\\n    )\\n    model.eval()\\n 5% winsorized mean\\n                winsor = scipy_stats.mstats.winsorize(arr, limits=[0.05, 0.05])\\n                winsorized_mean = float(np.mean(winsor))\\n                skewness = float(scipy_stats.skew(arr)) if len(arr) >= 3 else float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n                std_val = float(np.std(arr))\\n                outlier_count = int(np.sum(np.abs(arr - mean) > 3 * std_val)) if std_val > 0 else 0\\n                ii_out[pname][level_str] = {\\n                    \\\"mean\\\": round(mean, 6),\\n                    \\\"ci_low\\\": round(ci_low, 6),\\n                    \\\"ci_high\\\": round(ci_high, 6),\\n                    \\\"median\\\": round(median_val, 6),\\n                    \\\"winsorized_mean\\\": round(winsorized_mean, 6),\\n                    \\\"skewness\\\": round(skewness, 4),\\n                    \\\"outlier_count\\\": outlier_count,\\n                }\\n\\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\\n    # Pass / fail\\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\\n    pass_fail = evaluate_pass_fail(ii_out)\\n\\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\\n    # Assemble output\\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\\n    # P1: build sparsity histogram\\n    diagnostics_section: Dict[str, Any] = {\\n        \\\"per_problem_sparsity\\\": all_problem_diagnostics,\\n    }\\n    if all_sparsity_values:\\n        counts, bin_edges = np.histogram(all_sparsity_values, bins=50)\\n        diagnostics_section[\\\"global_sparsity_histogram\\\"] = {\\n            \\\"bins\\\": bin_edges.tolist(),\\n            \\\"counts\\\": counts.tolist(),\\n        }\\n\\n    output = {\\n        \\\"config\\\": {\\n            \\\"model\\\": args.model_path,\\n            \\\"sparsity_levels\\\": sparsity_levels,\\n            \\\"masking_method\\\": args.masking_method,\\n            \\\"head_importance_metric\\\": \\\"attention_frobenius_norm\\\" if use_importance else \\\"none\\\",\\n            \\\"kv_importance_metric\\\": \\\"cumulative_attention_received\\\" if use_importance else \\\"none\\\",\\n            \\\"segmentation_method\\\": \\\"quantile\\\",\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.52}}"}

Now I have a clear understanding of the code structure. Let me identify the key points:

1. **Problem loop**: `for idx, item in enumerate(data):` around line ~240
2. **Phase segmentation**: Each problem gets segmented into phases (execution/reasoning/transition), and each phase is a segment
3. **II computation**: Inside the loop, for each `(level, phase)` pair, `ii_val` is computed and appended to `ii_per_phase_level[phase.name][level_str]`
4. **Current accumulation**: `ii_per_phase_level` is a flat dict of `{phase_name: {level_str: [ii_values]}}` where each ii_value is one **segment** from one problem

{
  "server": "westd-22162",
  "command": "cat > /tmp/patch_profiling.py << 'PYEOF'\nimport re\n\nfilepath = \"/root/autodl-tmp/pacs/pacs/profiling/run_profiling.py\"\nwith open(filepath, \"r\") as f:\n    code = f.read()\n\n# Patch 1: Add per_problem_ii_data accumulator after all_sparsity_values\nold1 = \"    all_sparsity_values: List[float] = []\"\nnew1 = \"\"\"    all_sparsity_values: List[float] = []\n\n    # Per-problem II accumulator (keyed by level_str)\n    per_problem_ii_data: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)\"\"\"\nassert old1 in code, \"Patch 1 target not found\"\ncode = code.replace(old1, new1, 1)\n\n# Patch 2: Add per-problem tracking inside the problem loop.\n# Insert problem_phase_ii initialization right before the level loop\nold2 = \"        # --- C2 fix: generate masks once per level, reuse across phases ---\"\nnew2 = \"\"\"        # Per-problem II tracking\n        problem_phase_ii: Dict[str, Dict[str, List[float]]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))\n\n        # --- C2 fix: generate masks once per level, reuse across phases ---\"\"\"\nassert old2 in code, \"Patch 2 target not found\"\ncode = code.replace(old2, new2, 1)\n\n# Patch 3: After ii_per_phase_level append, also append to problem_phase_ii\nold3 = \"                ii_per_phase_level[phase.name][level_str].append(ii_val)\"\nnew3 = \"\"\"                ii_per_phase_level[phase.name][level_str].append(ii_val)\n                    problem_phase_ii[level_str][phase.name].append(ii_val)\"\"\"\nassert old3 in code, \"Patch 3 target not found\"\ncode = code.replace(old3, new3, 1)\n\n# Patch 4: After the logger.info for phases (end of per-problem block), \n# compute per-problem medians\nold4 = '''        logger.info(\n            \"  -> %d phases, boundaries at %s\",\n            len(annotation.phases), annotation.boundaries,\n        )'''\nnew4 = '''        logger.info(\n            \"  -> %d phases, boundaries at %s\",\n            len(annotation.phases), annotation.boundaries,\n        )\n\n        # Build per-problem II record (median across segments within each phase)\n        for level in sparsity_levels:\n            level_str = str(level)\n            record = {\"problem_idx\": idx, \"problem_id\": problem_id}\n            for pname_short, pname_full in [(\"high\", \"high_sparsity\"), (\"mid\", \"mid_sparsity\"), (\"low\", \"low_sparsity\")]:\n                vals = problem_phase_ii[level_str].get(pname_full, [])\n                record[f\"ii_{pname_short}\"] = float(np.median(vals)) if vals else None\n                record[f\"n_segments_{pname_short}\"] = len(vals)\n            per_problem_ii_data[level_str].append(record)'''\nassert old4 in code, \"Patch 4 target not found\"\ncode = code.replace(old4, new4, 1)\n\n# Patch 5: Add per_problem_ii to output dict\nold5 = '''        \"warnings\": warnings_list,\n        \"diagnostics\": diagnostics_section,'''\nnew5 = '''        \"warnings\": warnings_list,\n        \"diagnostics\": diagnostics_section,\n        \"per_problem_ii\": dict(per_problem_ii_data),'''\nassert old5 in code, \"Patch 5 target not found\"\ncode = code.replace(old5, new5, 1)\n\nwith open(filepath, \"w\") as f:\n    f.write(code)\n\nprint(\"All 5 patches applied successfully\")\nPYEOF\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python /tmp/patch_profiling.py",
  "account": "root"
}

The `per_problem_ii` field is there, but the values are NaN! That means the `problem_phase_ii` accumulator is collecting NaN values. Let me check why.

Looking at the code flow:
1. For each phase in annotation.phases, we compute `ii_val`
2. We append to `ii_per_phase_level[phase.name][level_str]`
3. We append to `problem_phase_ii[level_str][phase.name]`
4. After the loop, we take median of `problem_phase_ii[level_str][pname_full]`


5. The aggregate output shows valid II values exist, so the issue might be that the phase names in `annotation.phases` don't match the expected sparsity labels like "high_sparsity", "mid_sparsity", "low_sparsity".

Looking at the dry-run output, the phase names ARE correct — high_sparsity has 8 segments, low_sparsity has 5, and mid_sparsity has 10. The segment counts align with what's being logged, so the naming isn't the problem. Let me trace through the actual calculation logic to see where the NaN values are coming from.

I'm checking the condition that computes the interaction information — it divides by the dense perplexity, and if that's zero, it returns NaN. The aggregate mean isn't NaN though, which suggests the issue might be in how values are being collected or appended to the per-phase results versus the aggregate results. me re-read the patched code to verify it's handling the calculations correctly. Both branches should process valid and NaN values the same way.

But I'm noticing something odd—the output shows the correct segment counts but all ii_val entries are NaN, yet the aggregate mean is positive, which shouldn't be possible if all values were NaN. Looking at the bootstrap filtering code, it strips out NaN values before computing the aggregate, so the aggregate only reflects valid entries. But for the per-problem median calculation, even a single NaN value in a phase would propagate through and return NaN for the entire result.

The real issue is likely that I'm using `np.median` instead of `np.nanmedian`—the former returns NaN if any input is NaN, while the latter ignores NaN values. Let me trace through the problem_phase_ii population logic to see where the NaN values are coming from in the first place. I see the issue now—`np.median` returns NaN when any value in the array is NaN, but since the aggregate means are valid, there are definitely some non-NaN values in those lists. Switching to `np.nanmedian` will skip the NaN values and give me the actual median of the valid data points.

{
  "server": "westd-22162",
  "command": "cat > /root/autodl-tmp/pacs/analysis/bootstrap_per_problem.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"\nPer-problem non-parametric bootstrap for phase gradient CI.\nResampling unit: problem (n=50), NOT segment.\nMethod: percentile bootstrap, B=10000.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport argparse\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\ndef load_per_problem_ii(results_path, level_str=None):\n    with open(results_path) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    ppi = data[\"per_problem_ii\"]\n    if level_str is not None:\n        return ppi[level_str]\n    levels = list(ppi.keys())\n    if len(levels) == 1:\n        return ppi[levels[0]]\n    raise ValueError(f\"Multiple levels found: {levels}. Specify --level.\")\n\n\ndef bootstrap_gradient(per_problem_ii, n_bootstrap=10000, ci_level=0.95, seed=42):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    ii_high = np.array([p[\"ii_high\"] for p in per_problem_ii if p[\"ii_high\"] is not None])\n    ii_mid = np.array([p[\"ii_mid\"] for p in per_problem_ii if p[\"ii_mid\"] is not None])\n    ii_low = np.array([p[\"ii_low\"] for p in per_problem_ii if p[\"ii_low\"] is not None])\n\n    n = min(len(ii_high), len(ii_mid), len(ii_low))\n    ii_high = ii_high[:n]\n    ii_mid = ii_mid[:n]\n    ii_low = ii_low[:n]\n\n    point_gradient = np.median(ii_low) / np.median(ii_high)\n\n    gradients = np.empty(n_bootstrap)\n    for i in range(n_bootstrap):\n        idx = rng.randint(0, n, size=n)\n        med_low = np.median(ii_low[idx])\n        med_high = np.median(ii_high[idx])\n        gradients[i] = med_low / med_high if med_high != 0 else np.nan\n\n    gradients = gradients[~np.isnan(gradients)]\n    alpha = 1 - ci_level\n    ci_lo = np.percentile(gradients, 100 * alpha / 2)\n    ci_hi = np.percentile(gradients, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2))\n    p_value = float(np.mean(gradients <= 1.0))\n\n    return {\n        \"gradient\": float(point_gradient),\n        \"ci_low\": float(ci_lo),\n        \"ci_high\": float(ci_hi),\n        \"p_value\": p_value,\n        \"n_problems\": n,\n        \"n_bootstrap\": len(gradients),\n        \"median_ii_high\": float(np.median(ii_high)),\n        \"median_ii_mid\": float(np.median(ii_mid)),\n        \"median_ii_low\": float(np.median(ii_low)),\n    }\n\n\ndef bootstrap_phase_means(per_problem_ii, n_bootstrap=10000, ci_level=0.95, seed=42):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    results = {}\n    for phase in [\"high\", \"mid\", \"low\"]:\n        vals = np.array([p[f\"ii_{phase}\"] for p in per_problem_ii if p[f\"ii_{phase}\"] is not None])\n        n = len(vals)\n        if n < 2:\n            results[phase] = {\"median\": None, \"ci_low\": None, \"ci_high\": None, \"n\": n}\n            continue\n        boot_medians = np.empty(n_bootstrap)\n        for i in range(n_bootstrap):\n            idx = rng.randint(0, n, size=n)\n            boot_medians[i] = np.median(vals[idx])\n        alpha = 1 - ci_level\n        results[phase] = {\n            \"median\": float(np.median(vals)),\n            \"ci_low\": float(np.percentile(boot_medians, 100 * alpha / 2)),\n            \"ci_high\": float(np.percentile(boot_medians, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2))),\n            \"n\": n,\n        }\n    return results\n\n\nEXPERIMENTS = {\n    \"importance_s015\": {\n        \"path\": \"profiling_output_v6_importance_s015/profiling_results.json\",\n        \"strategy\": \"importance\", \"sparsity\": \"0.15\",\n    },\n    \"importance_s020\": {\n        \"path\": \"profiling_output_v6_importance_s020/profiling_results.json\",\n        \"strategy\": \"importance\", \"sparsity\": \"0.20\",\n    },\n    \"importance_s025\": {\n        \"path\": \"profiling_output_v6_importance_s025/profiling_results.json\",\n        \"strategy\": \"importance\", \"sparsity\": \"0.25\",\n    },\n    \"importance_s030\": {\n        \"path\": \"profiling_output_v6_importance_s030/profiling_results.json\",\n        \"strategy\": \"importance\", \"sparsity\": \"0.30\",\n    },\n    \"random_s015\": {\n        \"path\": \"profiling_output_v6_random_s015/profiling_results.json\",\n        \"strategy\": \"random\", \"sparsity\": \"0.15\",\n    },\n    \"random_s025\": {\n        \"path\": \"profiling_output_v6_random_s025/profiling_results.json\",\n        \"strategy\": \"random\", \"sparsity\": \"0.25\",\n    },\n}\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--base_dir\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/pacs\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_bootstrap\", type=int, default=10000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output\", default=None)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    base = Path(args.base_dir)\n    all_results = {}\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*90}\")\n    print(f\"Per-Problem Bootstrap Analysis (n_bootstrap={args.n_bootstrap}, resampling unit=problem)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*90}\")\n    print(f\"\\n{'Strategy':<12} {'Sparsity':<10} {'Gradient':<10} {'95% CI':<22} {'p-value':<10} {'n':<5} \"\n          f\"{'II_high':<10} {'II_mid':<10} {'II_low':<10}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 100)\n\n    for name, cfg in EXPERIMENTS.items():\n        fpath = base / cfg[\"path\"]\n        if not fpath.exists():\n            print(f\"{cfg['strategy']:<12} {cfg['sparsity']:<10} {'[NOT FOUND]':<10}\")\n            continue\n        ppi = load_per_problem_ii(str(fpath))\n        grad = bootstrap_gradient(ppi, n_bootstrap=args.n_bootstrap)\n        phase_ci = bootstrap_phase_means(ppi, n_bootstrap=args.n_bootstrap)\n\n        ci_str = f\"[{grad['ci_low']:.2f}, {grad['ci_high']:.2f}]\"\n        p_str = f\"< 0.001\" if grad[\"p_value\"] < 0.001 else f\"{grad['p_value']:.3f}\"\n        print(f\"{cfg['strategy']:<12} {cfg['sparsity']:<10} {grad['gradient']:<10.2f} {ci_str:<22} \"\n              f\"{p_str:<10} {grad['n_problems']:<5} \"\n              f\"{grad['median_ii_high']:<10.4f} {grad['median_ii_mid']:<10.4f} {grad['median_ii_low']:<10.4f}\")\n\n        all_results[name] = {\n            \"strategy\": cfg[\"strategy\"],\n            \"sparsity\": cfg[\"sparsity\"],\n            \"gradient\": grad,\n            \"phase_ci\": phase_ci,\n        }\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*90}\")\n    print(\"\\nPer-phase median II with 95% CI (bootstrap over problems):\")\n    print(f\"{'Strategy':<12} {'Sparsity':<10} {'Phase':<8} {'Median II':<12} {'95% CI':<22} {'n':<5}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 70)\n    for name, res in all_results.items():\n        for phase in [\"high\", \"mid\", \"low\"]:\n            pc = res[\"phase_ci\"][phase]\n            if pc[\"median\"] is None:\n                continue\n            ci_str = f\"[{pc['ci_low']:.4f}, {pc['ci_high']:.4f}]\"\n            print(f\"{res['strategy']:<12} {res['sparsity']:<10} {phase:<8} {pc['median']:<12.4f} {ci_str:<22} {pc['n']:<5}\")\n\n    print(f\"{'='*90}\\n\")\n\n    out_path = args.output or str(base / \"analysis\" / \"bootstrap_results.json\")\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(all_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"Results saved to {out_path}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF\necho \"bootstrap script written\"",
  "account": "root"
}

Average ~2 min per problem, so 50 problems ≈ 100 minutes.
